# Food catalog (generated)

Generated by `python3 tools/food_catalog.py` from the `/dumpfood` export of 2026-11-08 21:14: 1702 edible items, 1663 typed, 40 excluded. Don't edit this file: types live in [food_types.json](../kubejs/generation_scripts/food/food_types.json), values in the mods (and later the tuning config). How it all fits together: [food.md](food.md).

**Columns.** Hunger: nutrition, in half shanks. Mod: saturation modifier. Sat: saturation points added (hunger x mod x 2, never more than the food bar). SoL: Spice of Life complexity now, what one eat adds to food diversity before the age penalty (`*` = set in the SoL config, otherwise its own formula). Canned: the Canned Goods can made from it. Flags: M meat, A always edible, F fast (16 ticks instead of 32), D drink animation. Effects: level, seconds, and the chance when under 100%. Leaves: item handed back.

## Base ingredients: recipe chains

One row per raw base (every raw/* food that isn't a cut, plus the inedible raw inputs of cooking and cutting recipes). Compressed: 9 (or 4) into one storage block. Cooked: furnace, smoker or campfire. Sliced: cutting board or Create saw. Sliced, cooked: a cooked slice, from either direction. Values are hunger/mod. Used in: recipes that take it directly or through a tag.

## Canned Goods: can vs what goes in

Every can is 2 of its source plus an iron ingot, and gives the empty can back. Parity means a can is worth what went into it: "2x source" is the hunger and saturation points of the two sources eaten one after the other. Sat pts are uncapped here, so values above 20 show how far past the food bar a can goes.
